## Exact prospective design (historical proposal, not authorization)
Prospective FFE-V2 opportunity-window hypothesis HELD (one fixed curriculum treatment; no implementation/runtime authorization): more actual eligible first and repeat opportunities within each assigned chronological band can improve both-loaded visitation without forced commands or modifying physical/reward semantics. Retain fixed p1/8 cohort, four fixed band/cell draws, original independent20% nominal and untouched ordinary branch, original direction/magnitude, max2/episode, natural reset/closure and absolute clock, exact actual load and capture thresholds, causal standing coherence and no fallback. Candidate timing only: retain due T in original [40,80]+160*b, extend first search from T through 112+160*b inclusive; repeat search all gaps30..40 inclusive (instead of 9-tick window starting at gap30..32), last possible repeat152+160*b (< chunk boundary160+160*b; last k632, only7 subsequent epoch ticks, explicitly censor tails). Existing independent gap RNG key may be retained unused in V2 only; no extra native RNG split. No extra reset, command forcing/holding, action override, reward shaping, replay, state teleport or proxy relaxation. This is a prospective engineering proposal, not selected numerical optimum. Longer hard blocking/tail censor may impair ordinary exposure or learning; same frozen floors and tail accounting must reveal this. If later evidence shows no useful both-loaded joint occupancy, do not automatically widen gaps, increase cohort/budget or adjust load/capture thresholds. Do not conflate additional first events with adequate target repeats.

Before any approved V2 integration freeze a compact per-band/cell/half risk-set ledger in existing environment/instrumenter/trainer only: assigned and actual due opportunities, first at-risk rows and unique lanes with each gate (standing coherence, source clock, boundary/nominal, valid geometry, count, loaded), repeat at-risk rows and unique first-event lanes with mode1/2/3/0, D>=.10, joint mode3&D>=.10 and all other predicates together, chronological gap bins30..40, actual injection and per-stage closure reasons incl terminal/refresh/expiry/censor; overlap counts or explicitly ordered funnel, not independent marginal multiplication. Report counts of rows AND unique lanes, actual committed PPO rows only, and post-event tail/safety at matched available denominators. Guard checks exact decision replay, valid geometry, legacy event conservation, reward bit identity, no SGD/normalizer mutation on invalid batch. Reuse source geometry/no extra physics or inference, typed reset projection, fixed provenance and separate namespaces. All original 24 reward, physics/PD/obs/action/termination/network/optimizer/DR and immutable Official CTRL typed fullstate9b19312d, same5242880 train/800 optimizer/2000 diagnostic/20 rollouts/4 chunks if later approved. Frozen half-cell>=3 each, chunk>=1, nominal>=916743, broad1256/1262/1360/1296/1344 and legacy gates unchanged. Falsifier: missed joint exposure or ordinary/nominal floor, technical STOP on malformed evidence; no retry. Controlled science remains separate +2/8 standing double survival AND recovery5 and all nominal/moving/safety gates. Existing FFE-V1 exposure result never changes.

Exact context and constraints: [v1.2.294 L3778–3791](https://github.com/nguyenbui5701/G1_PAPER_REPRO/blob/a2a1056075f4a41e9f05115b30e2a50144b34154/G1_RESEARCH_RULES.md#L3778-L3791). **Evidence and falsifiable hypothesis.** V1 hard1022 / first144 / repeat30, assigned single-loaded25/77 vs both-loaded5/67 first lanes, aggregate expiry780; stand double survival and recovery5 CTRL6/8 vs FFE5/8; 34 hard entries/23 cases. Sources: immutable [L3778–3791](https://github.com/nguyenbui5701/G1_PAPER_REPRO/blob/a2a1056075f4a41e9f05115b30e2a50144b34154/G1_RESEARCH_RULES.md#L3778-L3791), [L3980–3989](https://github.com/nguyenbui5701/G1_PAPER_REPRO/blob/a2a1056075f4a41e9f05115b30e2a50144b34154/G1_RESEARCH_RULES.md#L3980-L3989). H1: more first opportunities and all gap30..40 opportunities increase actual qualified repeat visitation without sacrificing nominal/broad exposure. H2 (separate scientific hypothesis): sufficient exposure with original learning signal improves standing double survival AND recovery5 without moving/safety loss. H1 falsified for this candidate by frozen material gate FAIL; H2 judged only by separately authorized controlled DEV, never by exposure or training reward. A ledger can identify scheduler predicate bottlenecks on observed trajectories, not physical causes or counterfactual learning. No numeric optimum asserted.

### Fixed treatment and immutable controls
- Use the preceding exact prospective timing design, inclusive first T..112+160*b and repeat first_k+30..first_k+40. First window 33..73 ticks; repeat 11 ticks. At first eligible tick inject exactly once; no lookahead, fallback, forcing, resampling, new RNG split, added reset or post-result tuning. Initial due T remains uniform integer 40..80+160*b. Preserve independent legacy gap draw 30..32 on existing fold-in tag for passive shadow labeling; V2 scheduling uses gap30, not the sampled gap, for its repeat-due clock; gap draw remains unchanged telemetry. Distinguish sampled gap from scheduled earliest gap and actual gap. Do not change native random draws. Preserve legacy scheduler due/pending conservation with actual V2 schedule, not shadow labels.
- Retain hard cohort p=.125, four fixed cell/band draws, independent nominal20%, max2/episode, direction/magnitude, exact natural boundary/refresh/terminal/invalid closures, original broad branch when unblocked and no fallback. First eligible: active FIRST, within window, !boundary,!nominal, coherent live standing, valid geometry, source_step<=459, count0, mode>0. Repeat: active REPEAT, within window, !boundary,!nominal, coherent, source_step<500, valid geometry, count1, target mode, native float32 cost>=.5. Keep original reason precedence, deadline inclusive; post-transition terminal first counts as actual first but does not create repeat risk set. No change in behavior outside approved window timing. Treat actual float32 cost predicate as authoritative; D>=.10 only explanatory for loaded valid geometry, never substitute it or round near threshold; mode0 cost1 is not evidence of large D. If D is exposed, reuse the existing geometric computation, no second native step/sample.
- Keep original 24 rewards bit-identical, PPO network/optimizer/normalizer/GAE, observations/actions, physics/PD/DR/termination, pinned CUDA sources, typed full-state parent Official CTRL9b19312d, no FFE-V1 warm start. Budget if separately approved: 8192 lanes ×32 ticks ×20 rollouts=5242880 transitions, 800 optimizer updates, 4 chunks of5 rollouts, 2000 diagnostic evaluation transitions; exact existing frozen reset/provenance and resource contracts. No extra native inference or transitions for ledger. Archived Official CTRL primary and M12 descriptive, no rerun.

### Risk-set ledger (passive, versioned distinct schema)
Observation unit: synchronized continuation lane (stable ID 0..8191) × absolute k (0..639), actual pre-injection state and corresponding post outcome; fixed assigned band b and cell j, chronological half floor(b/2); report actual observed chunk as well for tails. One lane has at most one hard assignment and one first/repeat opportunity per continuation. Natural episode reset does not create a new hard lane. All counters from actual unscaled committed PPO rows; rejected/uncommitted rows only in separate abort evidence. No observation/reward/gradient inputs or new control feedback.

1. First risk set: due assigned, at-risk FIRST predecision rows inclusive T..deadline (including closure row), separately count unique assigned lanes, due lanes, rows/lanes at risk, eligible rows/lanes, actual first event and terminal-first. Use explicit initial-state handling and hard/active phase, not post-phase alone. Record boundary, nominal, live standing coherence, clock<=459, count0, geometry validity, mode>0 and exact full conjunction; pre-window closures and post-window/no-row censors reported separately. Geometry invalid in active decision path is technical STOP, not a benign exposure miss.
2. Repeat risk set: actual first which opened repeat (distinct from all first); include all live waiting rows before earliest gap to account for early closure, separately at-risk decision rows at actual gap30..40, including closure row. Denominator also report all first-event lanes by assigned target cell (comparable to V1's 25/77 and5/67); then opened repeat, reached window, eligible, actual repeat. Track gap bins 30..40, boundary/nominal, coherent standing, source_step<500, count1, valid geometry, mode0/1/2/3, exact native cost>=.5, target-mode match and conjunction. Record mode × cost joint table for geometry-valid rows, D threshold joint with mode3 only if exact existing D is available, and conjunction with every other gate. Never count no-load cost1 as both-loaded. Count support transitions and pre/post outcome separately. Include joint failure bitmask for decision predicates (overlapping misses), plus deterministic ordered funnel with declared order boundary→nominal→coherence/clock→geometry→count→mode→cost; order is reporting only, not new scheduler precedence. Report per-predicate pass/fail and exact overlap or loss counts, not product of marginals.
3. Closure conservation: stage FIRST vs REPEAT, pre reason vs post reason, event vs miss (terminal event counts as event), exact precedence; split boundary, nominal, deadline/expiry, coherence, source refresh, terminal, invalid and host/chunk censor as observable. Where multiple true, retain overlap and original canonical code; distinguish refresh vs nonrefresh coherence using existing flags, do not invent causes. At assigned band end no unresolved FIRST/REPEAT for that band, future assignments and pending tails explicitly accounted. Reconcile assigned = first events + first misses + pending, opened repeats = repeat events + repeat misses + pending, first events = opened + nonopened; use carry in/out at chunk edges, no double count. Pre-window closures kept distinct from eligible opportunity misses. Host reset/tail censors never called recovery success.
4. Shadow windows: label first rows in V1 T..T+8 vs V2-only; repeat rows in original sampled gap..gap+8 relative to actual first vs V2-only. Report rows/unique lanes/eligible rows/actual injection in mutually exclusive window labels and intersection; count first/repeat injections outside V1 labels. Only evaluate available observed predecision states; after V2 injection/closure or V1 hypothetical deadline no unobserved alternate states invented. V1 gap draw passive. Track last comparable point and label same-trajectory predicate opportunity, not V1 counterfactual, not matched causal estimate or complete shadow scheduler. Never use shadow opportunities as material exposure events.
5. Tail/safety: by event kind, assigned cell, actual event band, available horizon and stratum, track post-event executed/survived/same-command at existing windows1/10/25/50/100/250 ticks, terminal/refresh/reset and final right censor, joint/control/force excess, foot-foot and support/capture evolution on available states. Use existing tail carry; if next push supersedes first tail, close explicitly. Event at k632 has only7 later epoch rows; do not credit missing horizons or compare unequal exposure. Report actual denominators and censored counts. Source pre sample at k+1 is only a valid successor if same live episode; do not label reset geometry post-recovery. No extra physics/inference.
6. Counts/identity: exact integral counters; row partitions and subset/monotonic checks, unique lane OR across rollouts/chunks via fixed bounded passive bitsets or bool carry indexed by lane (not sum of per-rollout unique counts); persist carry at chunk boundary and reset only at approved continuation edge. Include per-band/cell/half and gap distributions with clear unique union semantics across overlapping predicates. No raw full 5M-row dump; budget telemetry and report size, bound device buffers and host metrics, compare to frozen VRAM/storage/watchdog constraints; concrete size estimate and exact schema/field dtypes required in held integration before static GO. Fail closed if cannot fit; no budget increase authorized.

```python
# H4FFE2_CANDIDATE_STATIC_OWNER_BEGIN
import hashlib, json, os, selectors, shutil, signal, subprocess, sys, time
from pathlib import Path

root = Path.cwd().resolve()
runs = root / "m463_runs"
log = runs / "m470_h4ffe2_static_candidate_oneuse_v1.log"
out = runs / "m470_h4_m20_ffe_v2"
cap = 16 * 1024**2
footer_reserve = 4096
def require(ok, msg):
    if not ok:
        raise RuntimeError(msg)
def git(*args):
    return subprocess.check_output(["git", *args], cwd=root).decode().strip()
require(git("branch", "--show-current") == "experiment/b1-hardware-scaled", "BRANCH")
require(git("status", "--porcelain", "--untracked-files=no") == "", "TRACKED_DIRTY")
require(git("rev-parse", "HEAD^") == "ee1f7eeeae560ecccfa19f9157a1ba8b4da34bc2", "REVIEW_PARENT")
for name, blob in (
    ("m463_coverage_env.py", "efe71d850746f07c42f5d1e0256399d2b963da21"),
    ("m463_instrument_brax.py", "6b28d055e068f9a36f7b58f852167fbc113a8b54"),
    ("m463_push_ppo_train.py", "7a4c95ab00b072ab00764f86e0b7221561b97dad"),
    ("G1_RESEARCH_RULES.md", "7d5961158420ad2053ed00cc1342aa2a54a12ce9")):
    require(git("hash-object", name) == blob, "SOURCE_PIN " + name)
require(runs.is_dir() and not runs.is_symlink(), "RUNS_PATH")
require(not out.exists() and not out.is_symlink(), "RUNTIME_OUTPUT_PRESENT")
require(shutil.disk_usage(runs).free >= 2*1024**3 + 512*1024**2 + cap, "DISK")
head = git("rev-parse", "HEAD")
argv = [sys.executable, "-B", "-u", str(root/"m463_push_ppo_train.py"),
    "--h4ffe2-static",
    "--checkpoint", "m430_seed1/checkpoint_pilot_20261114_120515_1722937_step_401080320.msgpack",
    "--restore-report", "m462_runs/typed_restore_20261120_162901.json",
    "--reward-report", "m463_runs/m470_h3_m1_2_independent_10M_20261124_183319_225465810/report.json"]
rc = None
error = None
overflow = False
stored = 0
child = None
deadline = time.monotonic() + 3600
# Exclusive fixed log is the one-use claim. Never remove/rename/reuse.
with log.open("xb") as stream:
    def emit(data):
        global stored, overflow
        room = cap - footer_reserve - stored
        keep = data[:max(0, room)]
        if keep:
            stream.write(keep)
            stream.flush()
            stored += len(keep)
        if len(keep) != len(data):
            overflow = True
            raise RuntimeError("LOG_CAP")
    try:
        emit(("H4FFE2_CANDIDATE_STATIC_HEAD " + head + "\n").encode())
        child = subprocess.Popen(argv, cwd=root, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
            stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, start_new_session=True, bufsize=0)
        with selectors.DefaultSelector() as sel:
            sel.register(child.stdout, selectors.EVENT_READ)
            while True:
                left = deadline - time.monotonic()
                require(left > 0, "WATCHDOG")
                if not sel.select(timeout=min(1, left)):
                    continue
                data = os.read(child.stdout.fileno(), 65536)
                if not data:
                    break
                emit(data)
        rc = child.wait(timeout=max(.01, deadline-time.monotonic()))
        require(rc == 0, "STATIC_RC")
        require(git("rev-parse", "HEAD") == head and git("status", "--porcelain", "--untracked-files=no") == "", "POST_SOURCE")
        require(not out.exists() and not out.is_symlink(), "POST_RUNTIME_OUTPUT")
        lines = log.read_text(encoding="utf8").splitlines()
        require(lines[-1:] == ["H4FFE2_HELD_STATIC_CANDIDATE_PASS"] and lines.count("H4FFE2_HELD_STATIC_CANDIDATE_PASS") == 1, "CANDIDATE_RECEIPT")
        require("H4FFE2_STATIC_ZERO physical_transitions=0 resets=0 optimizer_updates=0 native_inference=0" in lines, "ZERO_RECEIPT")
        require("H4FFE2_FULL_STATIC_PASS" not in lines, "UNEXPECTED_FULL_STATIC")
    except BaseException as exc:
        error = repr(exc)[:1024]
        if child is not None and child.poll() is None:
            try:
                os.killpg(child.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
            except ProcessLookupError:
                pass
            rc = child.wait(timeout=10)
        elif child is not None:
            rc = child.returncode
    finally:
        status = dict(decision="CANDIDATE_COMPLETE" if rc == 0 and error is None and not overflow else "STOP",
            static_rc=rc, overflow=overflow, error=error, stored_child_and_header_bytes=stored)
        footer = ("\nH4FFE2_CANDIDATE_STATIC_STATUS " + json.dumps(status, ensure_ascii=True, sort_keys=True) + "\n").encode()
        require(len(footer) <= footer_reserve, "FOOTER_CAP")
        stream.write(footer)
        stream.flush()
        os.fsync(stream.fileno())
    require(stream.tell() <= cap, "FINAL_LOG_CAP")
print("===== STATIC RECEIPT TAIL =====", flush=True)
with log.open("rb") as f:
    f.seek(max(0, log.stat().st_size-16384))
    print(f.read().decode("utf8", errors="replace"), end="", flush=True)
print("H4FFE2_CANDIDATE_STATIC_LOG", str(log), log.stat().st_size,
      hashlib.sha256(log.read_bytes()).hexdigest(), flush=True)
sys.exit(0 if status["decision"] == "CANDIDATE_COMPLETE" else 1)
# H4FFE2_CANDIDATE_STATIC_OWNER_END
```

```python
# H4FFE2_FINAL_STATIC_OWNER_BEGIN
import hashlib, json, os, selectors, shutil, signal, subprocess, sys, time
from pathlib import Path

root = Path.cwd().resolve()
runs = root / "m463_runs"
log = runs / "m470_h4ffe2_static_final_oneuse_v1.log"
out = runs / "m470_h4_m20_ffe_v2"
cap = 16 * 1024**2
footer_reserve = 4096
def require(ok, msg):
    if not ok:
        raise RuntimeError(msg)
def git(*args):
    return subprocess.check_output(["git", *args], cwd=root).decode().strip()
require(git("branch", "--show-current") == "experiment/b1-hardware-scaled", "BRANCH")
require(git("status", "--porcelain", "--untracked-files=no") == "", "TRACKED_DIRTY")
require(git("rev-parse", "HEAD^") == "29a9208d8e68250f9489ceec07b20a3369e0437c", "REVIEW_PARENT")
for name, blob in (
    ("m463_coverage_env.py", "efe71d850746f07c42f5d1e0256399d2b963da21"),
    ("m463_instrument_brax.py", "6b28d055e068f9a36f7b58f852167fbc113a8b54"),
    ("m463_push_ppo_train.py", "2f7ef6a387c205bfee8f3b500a06490b817f9f12"),
    ("G1_RESEARCH_RULES.md", "7d5961158420ad2053ed00cc1342aa2a54a12ce9")):
    require(git("hash-object", name) == blob, "SOURCE_PIN " + name)
require(runs.is_dir() and not runs.is_symlink(), "RUNS_PATH")
require(not out.exists() and not out.is_symlink(), "RUNTIME_OUTPUT_PRESENT")
candidate = runs / "m470_h4ffe2_static_candidate_oneuse_v1.log"
candidate_sha = "58a07796561115d3c570258684043fc994bd037761bcd44db0fc5ebc9d1902c5"
generated_sha = "9d20cdd77ac0e3330cb5f3d89df408ba150bc568f1bfa3d4ee2e6399bcba72cf"
require(candidate.is_file() and not candidate.is_symlink() and candidate.stat().st_size == 2032, "CANDIDATE_LOG")
require(hashlib.sha256(candidate.read_bytes()).hexdigest() == candidate_sha, "CANDIDATE_SHA")
require(not log.exists() and not log.is_symlink(), "FINAL_ONE_USE_ALREADY_CLAIMED")
require(shutil.disk_usage(runs).free >= 2*1024**3 + 512*1024**2 + cap, "DISK")
head = git("rev-parse", "HEAD")
argv = [sys.executable, "-B", "-u", str(root/"m463_push_ppo_train.py"),
    "--h4ffe2-final-static",
    "--checkpoint", "m430_seed1/checkpoint_pilot_20261114_120515_1722937_step_401080320.msgpack",
    "--restore-report", "m462_runs/typed_restore_20261120_162901.json",
    "--reward-report", "m463_runs/m470_h3_m1_2_independent_10M_20261124_183319_225465810/report.json"]
rc = None
error = None
overflow = False
stored = 0
child = None
deadline = time.monotonic() + 3600
# Exclusive fixed log is the one-use claim. Never remove/rename/reuse.
with log.open("xb") as stream:
    def emit(data):
        global stored, overflow
        room = cap - footer_reserve - stored
        keep = data[:max(0, room)]
        if keep:
            stream.write(keep)
            stream.flush()
            stored += len(keep)
        if len(keep) != len(data):
            overflow = True
            raise RuntimeError("LOG_CAP")
    try:
        emit(("H4FFE2_FINAL_STATIC_HEAD " + head + "\n").encode())
        child = subprocess.Popen(argv, cwd=root, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
            stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, start_new_session=True, bufsize=0)
        with selectors.DefaultSelector() as sel:
            sel.register(child.stdout, selectors.EVENT_READ)
            while True:
                left = deadline - time.monotonic()
                require(left > 0, "WATCHDOG")
                if not sel.select(timeout=min(1, left)):
                    continue
                data = os.read(child.stdout.fileno(), 65536)
                if not data:
                    break
                emit(data)
        rc = child.wait(timeout=max(.01, deadline-time.monotonic()))
        require(rc == 0, "STATIC_RC")
        require(git("rev-parse", "HEAD") == head and git("status", "--porcelain", "--untracked-files=no") == "", "POST_SOURCE")
        require(not out.exists() and not out.is_symlink(), "POST_RUNTIME_OUTPUT")
        lines = log.read_text(encoding="utf8").splitlines()
        require(lines[0] == "H4FFE2_FINAL_STATIC_HEAD " + head, "FINAL_HEAD")
        require(lines[-1:] == ["H4FFE2_FULL_STATIC_PASS"] and lines.count("H4FFE2_FULL_STATIC_PASS") == 1, "FINAL_RECEIPT")
        require(lines.count("H4FFE2_STATIC_ZERO physical_transitions=0 resets=0 optimizer_updates=0 native_inference=0") == 1, "ZERO_RECEIPT")
        require("H4FFE2_HELD_STATIC_CANDIDATE_PASS" not in lines, "UNEXPECTED_CANDIDATE")
        require(lines.count("H4FFE2_ACCEPTED_CANDIDATE " + candidate_sha) == 1, "CANDIDATE_BINDING")
        require(lines.count("H4FFE2_GENERATED " + generated_sha) == 1, "GENERATED_BINDING")
        require(lines.count("H4FFE2_REVERSE_SOURCE_PASS") == 1, "REVERSE_BINDING")
        import re
        sources = [x for x in lines if x.startswith("H4FFE2_SOURCE ")]
        require(len(sources) == 1 and re.fullmatch(r"H4FFE2_SOURCE [0-9a-f]{64}", sources[0]) is not None, "SOURCE_BINDING")
        for tag, expected in (
            ("H4FFE2_FINAL_GUARD_FIXTURES ", dict(receipt_positive=1, receipt_negative=13, marker_positive=1, marker_negative=5, native_calls=0)),
            ("H4FFE2_TYPED_FIXTURES ", dict(positive=2, negative=5, native_calls=0, typed_mock_only=True)),
            ("H4FFE2_RESOURCE_SCHEMA ", dict(atomic_risk_plus_external_bytes=80787968, flags=180, hist=575, other_cap=134217728, payload_bytes=3062976, peak_xla_verified=False, raw_extra_bytes=31457280, risk_json_upper_bytes=32005376)),
            ("H4FFE2_MATCHED_CONTROL ", dict(archived_broad=[1569,1577,1700,1620,1679], minimum_broad=[1256,1262,1360,1296,1344], nominal=916743))):
            hits = [x[len(tag):] for x in lines if x.startswith(tag)]
            require(len(hits) == 1, "FINAL_SECTION " + tag)
            obj = json.loads(hits[0])
            require(type(obj) is dict and set(obj) == set(expected) and all(type(obj[k]) is type(expected[k]) for k in expected) and obj == expected, "FINAL_SECTION_VALUE " + tag)
        require(not any(x.startswith(("H4FFE2_CORE_FIXTURES ", "H4FFE2_BATCH_FIXTURES ", "H4FFE2_READER_FIXTURES ", "H4FFE2_TERMINAL_FIXTURES ")) for x in lines), "UNAUTHORIZED_CANDIDATE_FIXTURE_REPEAT")
    except BaseException as exc:
        error = repr(exc)[:1024]
        if child is not None and child.poll() is None:
            try:
                os.killpg(child.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
            except ProcessLookupError:
                pass
            rc = child.wait(timeout=10)
        elif child is not None:
            rc = child.returncode
    finally:
        status = dict(decision="FULL_STATIC_COMPLETE" if rc == 0 and error is None and not overflow else "STOP",
            static_rc=rc, overflow=overflow, error=error, stored_child_and_header_bytes=stored)
        footer = ("\nH4FFE2_FINAL_STATIC_STATUS " + json.dumps(status, ensure_ascii=True, sort_keys=True) + "\n").encode()
        require(len(footer) <= footer_reserve, "FOOTER_CAP")
        stream.write(footer)
        stream.flush()
        os.fsync(stream.fileno())
    require(stream.tell() <= cap, "FINAL_LOG_CAP")
print("===== STATIC RECEIPT TAIL =====", flush=True)
with log.open("rb") as f:
    f.seek(max(0, log.stat().st_size-16384))
    print(f.read().decode("utf8", errors="replace"), end="", flush=True)
print("H4FFE2_FINAL_STATIC_LOG", str(log), log.stat().st_size,
      hashlib.sha256(log.read_bytes()).hexdigest(), flush=True)
sys.exit(0 if status["decision"] == "FULL_STATIC_COMPLETE" else 1)
# H4FFE2_FINAL_STATIC_OWNER_END
```

```python
# H4FFE2_DEV_STATIC_OWNER_BEGIN
import hashlib, json, os, re, selectors, shutil, signal, subprocess, sys, time
from pathlib import Path
root = Path.cwd().resolve()
runs = root / "m463_runs"
out = runs / "m470_h4_m21_1_ffe2_dev_v1"
runtime = runs / "m470_h4ffe2_dev_runtime_v1.log"
log = runs / "m470_h4ffe2_dev_static_v1.log"
cap = 16 * 1024**2
reserve = 4096
def require(ok, reason):
    if not ok:
        raise RuntimeError(reason)
def git(*args):
    return subprocess.check_output(["git", *args], cwd=root, text=True).strip()
def unused(p):
    return not p.exists() and not p.is_symlink()
def clean():
    return git("status", "--porcelain", "--untracked-files=no") == ""
def pins():
    require(git("hash-object", "m463_gate3_cp_eval.py") == "87f82602fb57e93e5e39e51f2a65b257946e109a", "EVALUATOR_PIN")
    require(git("hash-object", "G1_RESEARCH_RULES.md") == "e09c35a82f06a97fe5406ba36bb3fd9664913583", "ROOT_PIN")
    require(not any(re.match(r"^[A-Z0-9_]+_GO:", x) for x in (root/"G1_RESEARCH_RULES.md").read_text("utf8").splitlines()), "NATIVE_MARKER")
require(git("branch", "--show-current") == "experiment/b1-hardware-scaled", "BRANCH")
head = git("rev-parse", "HEAD")
require(git("rev-parse", "HEAD^") == "09e091a6e297210f35975687bd88e7f7c4dcb3e0", "REVIEW_PARENT")
require(clean(), "TRACKED_DIRTY")
pins()
require(runs.is_dir() and not runs.is_symlink(), "RUNS_PATH")
require(all(unused(p) for p in (out, runtime, log)), "ONE_USE_OR_RUNTIME_USED")
require(shutil.disk_usage(runs).free >= 2*1024**3 + cap, "STORAGE")
require(not any(k.split(".")[0] in ("jax","jaxlib","brax","mujoco","mujoco_playground") for k in sys.modules), "NATIVE_PREIMPORT")
argv = [sys.executable, "-B", "-u", str(root/"m463_gate3_cp_eval.py"), "--mode", "plan", "--h4ffe2-eval", "--h4ffe2-eval-static", "--output", str(out/"report.json")]
expected = dict(archive_clone="NO_GO", secondary_identity=True, marker_negatives=5, reader_negatives=13, training_exposure_negatives=2, disposition_fixtures=3, partial_positive=1, partial_negatives=3, launcher_negative=1, byte_projection=True, step_graph=True, cli_hold=5, physical_transitions=0, resets=0, optimizer_updates=0, inference=0)
rc = None
error = None
overflow = False
stored = 0
child = None
deadline = time.monotonic() + 3600
with log.open("xb") as f:
    def emit(data):
        global stored, overflow
        keep = data[:max(0, cap-reserve-stored)]
        if keep:
            f.write(keep)
            f.flush()
            stored += len(keep)
        if len(keep) != len(data):
            overflow = True
            raise RuntimeError("LOG_CAP")
    try:
        emit(("H4FFE2_DEV_STATIC_HEAD " + head + "\n").encode())
        child = subprocess.Popen(argv, cwd=root, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, start_new_session=True, bufsize=0)
        with selectors.DefaultSelector() as sel:
            sel.register(child.stdout, selectors.EVENT_READ)
            while True:
                left = deadline-time.monotonic()
                require(left > 0, "WATCHDOG")
                if not sel.select(min(1,left)):
                    continue
                data = os.read(child.stdout.fileno(), 65536)
                if not data:
                    break
                emit(data)
        left = deadline-time.monotonic()
        require(left > 0, "WATCHDOG")
        rc = child.wait(timeout=left)
        require(rc == 0, "STATIC_RC")
        require(clean() and git("rev-parse", "HEAD") == head, "POST_SOURCE")
        pins()
        require(unused(out) and unused(runtime), "POST_RUNTIME_OUTPUT")
        lines = log.read_text("utf8").splitlines()
        require(lines[-1:] == ["H4FFE2_DEV_FULL_STATIC_PASS"] and lines.count("H4FFE2_DEV_FULL_STATIC_PASS") == 1, "STATIC_PASS")
        for tag in ("SOURCE", "PROVENANCE", "LAUNCHER"):
            prefix = "H4FFE2_DEV_"+tag+" "
            hits = [x for x in lines if x.startswith(prefix)]
            require(len(hits)==1 and re.fullmatch(re.escape(prefix)+r"[0-9a-f]{64}",hits[0]) is not None, "STATIC_"+tag)
        prefix = "H4FFE2_DEV_STATIC_FIXTURES "
        hits = [x[len(prefix):] for x in lines if x.startswith(prefix)]
        require(len(hits)==1, "FIXTURE_SECTION")
        obj = json.loads(hits[0])
        require(type(obj) is dict and obj==expected and all(type(obj[k]) is type(v) for k,v in expected.items()), "FIXTURE_VALUES")
    except BaseException as exc:
        error = repr(exc)[:1024]
        if child is not None:
            try:
                os.killpg(child.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
            except ProcessLookupError:
                pass
            rc = child.wait(timeout=10)
    finally:
        ok = rc == 0 and error is None and not overflow
        if ok:
            footer = b"STATIC_EXIT_CODE=0 TEE_EXIT_CODE=0\n"
        else:
            footer = ("\nH4FFE2_DEV_STATIC_STOP "+json.dumps(dict(static_rc=rc,error=error,overflow=overflow,stored_bytes=stored),ensure_ascii=True,sort_keys=True)+"\n").encode()
        require(len(footer) <= reserve, "FOOTER_CAP")
        f.write(footer)
        f.flush()
        os.fsync(f.fileno())
    require(f.tell() <= cap, "LOG_CAP")
print("===== STATIC RECEIPT TAIL =====", flush=True)
with log.open("rb") as f:
    f.seek(max(0,log.stat().st_size-16384))
    print(f.read().decode("utf8",errors="replace"),end="",flush=True)
print("H4FFE2_DEV_STATIC_LOG",str(log),log.stat().st_size,hashlib.sha256(log.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),flush=True)
print("H4FFE2_DEV_STATIC_OUTER_RC",0 if ok else 1,flush=True)
sys.exit(0 if ok else 1)
# H4FFE2_DEV_STATIC_OWNER_END
```

Native first_contact=(old air>0)&(current_contact|last_contact), then air+=dt, raw airtime sum(min((air-.2)*first_contact,.3)), commands unused, reset air for actual contact after reward. Thus first_contact is filtered and not identical to 0->1 contact, can fire on row after lift-off. Negative raw air contribution if T<.2, cap positive .3 each. Native step reward=sum(weighted terms)*dt. Native evaluates phase before phase advance; DEV records post-phase. No named swing_foot_contact. No reward conflict is established by formula alone.

OFFLINE METHOD (descriptive, not exact recovered telemetry): shared 46-column schema; for each policy reconstruct logical trajectory from nominal rows1..299 + single suffix starting300; double uses first334 of single then double suffix starting335; never duplicate shared prefix as independent evidence. This is source-formula proxy, not recorded weighted reward; count cases with any negative per-foot contribution, not necessarily net-negative total or actual touchdown. Count actual touchdown separately 0->1; compare pre-event airborne foot first touchdown within25 ticks to nominal only if same foot also airborne immediately before nominal event and both touchdown observed within25. Compute UNMASKED phase fit E=exp(-sum((z-rz)^2)/.01) using previous row post-phase (reset [0,pi]), .15 height, mean E_push-E_nominal over common10 rows. Request-side candidate if small/misdirected target changes in relevant joints despite clear unclipped authority AND observed inadequate placement/load; falsified as sole explanation if substantial appropriate target change is clipped or not realized. Execution-side candidate if target response exists and signed clamp/realized joint mismatch precedes foot deficit, differentiate expected tracking dynamics vs hard limit; if raw/effective identical and realized responsive, simple clipping bottleneck weakened; no inference from one high error, absence of sampled force clip not proof no saturation. Placement/support candidate if contact early but poor relative placement/load/momentum or no effective transfer; positive comparison checks overlap. Gait conflict candidate if actual phase/air signals oppose observed useful stepping before deficit, compare event-local magnitudes against single and positive, not choose by reward sum; if no differential or only after failure, deprioritize. Passive telemetry cannot identify counterfactual intent or prove alternative feasible, positive association is not causal reward evidence; even if signals align no new PPO design without credible actual committed learning exposure. No arbitrary new thresholds; label OBSERVED/INFERRED/UNTESTED, mixed/unresolved allowed.
